via sorted `expandingKeys`\\n- Supports pattern trailers (lines 2711-2715)\\n- Supports wildcard patterns ending with * (lines 2717-2720)\\n- Supports directory patterns ending with / (lines resolution:\\n- Creates ModuleResolutionState with no NodeResolutionFeatures\\n- Tries two passes: TypeScript/Declaration first, then JavaScript/JSON\\n- For relative imports: resolves candidate file directly\\n- For non-relative imports: climbs ancestor directories (with cache lookups)\\n- Falls back to @types directories and typeRoots\\n- No package.json exports/imports support\\n\\n### Path directory\\n- **paths**: Maps module names to alternate locations\\n- **rootDirs**: Maps virtual project structure to runtime structure\\n- **traceResolution**: Enables diagnostic output\\n-
